Migrations Created\nNo new migration was required; the existing database infrastructure (`credit_rate_table`, `credit_reservations`, `usage_ledger`, and `billing_payments`) is fully modular and supports runtime rate adjustments. Rates were updated and locked directly in the authoritative database table.\n\n---\n\n### 11. Double-Charge Protection\n- Client requests pass a deterministic `idempotency_key` based on operation payload and identity.\n- Database enforces `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(p_idempotency_key, 0))` preventing simultaneous twin-request execution.\n- Button states disable immediately on click with loading indicators across all detector interfaces.\n- Reservations transition through explicit state machines (`reserved` → `committed` or `released`).\n\n---\n\n### 12. Failure / Refund Behavior\n- When an upstream API, Edge Function, or provider times out or errors, the frontend calls `finalizeReservationOnServer(resId, false, errorReason)` or `settle_client_reservation(resId, 'failed')`.\n- PostgreSQL restores the exact reserved credit quantity: `credits_balance = credits_balance + v_res.credits_reserved`.\n- A ledger entry with `operation='reservation_released'` and `ledger_type='refund'` is committed.\n- A second refund attempt on an already released reservation evaluates `status NOT IN ('reserved', 'pending')` and returns `false`, preventing double refunds.\n\n---\n\n### 13. Paystack Findings\n- **Secret Key Security**: `PAYSTACK_SECRET_KEY` remains strictly server-side inside Supabase Edge Functions (`paystack-checkout`, `paystack-webhook`, `verify-paystack-payment`).\n- **Signature Verification**: Webhooks enforce HMAC-SHA512 verification of header `x-paystack-signature`.\n- **Server-Side Verification**: Payment status is fetched directly from `https://api.paystack.co/transaction/verify/${ref}`. Client-supplied amounts and user IDs are ignored; plan and amount are matched against server table `plan_prices`.\n- **Idempotency**: `apply_verified_subscription_payment` uses `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(p_provider||':'||p_payment_reference,0))` and checks `billing_payments` to ensure duplicate webhook deliveries return `idempotent_replay: true` without granting credits twice.\n\n---\n\n### 14. Security Findings\n- Users cannot manipulate deductions: credit calculations and balance mutations take place exclusively inside PostgreSQL `SECURITY DEFINER` RPCs.\n- Negative balances are mathematically impossible: `v_user_balance >= v_effective_cost` is evaluated before any deduction, accompanied by row-level locks on `profiles`.\n- Plan gating enforces `billing_plan_rank(profile.plan) >= billing_plan_rank(rate.min_plan)` preventing unpaid users from executing Pro-only tools.\n\n---\n\n### 15. Tests Performed\n- **Live PostgreSQL Transaction Tests**: Real PL/pgSQL test block executed in the database verifying:\n  - AI Detector: 10 → 9 credits\n  - Humanizer: 20 → 10 credits\n  - Plagiarism: 20 → 10 credits\n  - Image Detector: 10 → 5 credits\n  - Voice Detector: 10 → 5 credits\n  - Deepfake Detector: 20 → 10 credits\n  - Video Detector: 20 → 5 credits\n  - Citation / Hallucination: 10 → 5 credits\n  - Insufficient Balance: Balance 9 on Humanizer (10 cr needed) → rejected with `allowed = false`, balance preserved at 9.\n  - Failure Rollback: 20 → reserve 10 (balance 10) → failure → balance restored to 20; double refund rejected.\n- **Unit & Integration Test Suite**: Complete Vitest run across 74 test files and 802 test cases.\n- **Static Code Analysis**: `npm run lint` across 747 files with 0 errors.\n\n---\n\n### 16. Billing Defects Discovered\n1. **Rate Table Discrepancies**: `credit_rate_table` and `RATE_TABLE` held legacy rates (Humanizer 3 cr, Plagiarism 2 cr, Image 2 cr, Video 2–6 cr, Voice 2 cr).\n2. **Missing Atomic Client Reservation on Standalone Tools**: Several media analysis pages (`CitationVerifier.tsx`, `HallucinationDetector.tsx`, `AIVoiceDetector.tsx`, `DeepfakeDetector.tsx`) executed scans without establishing an atomic reservation with the central RPC, relying on asynchronous client-side deduction.\n3. **Edge Function Hardcoded Cost Defaults**: Edge Functions for `plagiarism-checker` and `run-humanizer` had legacy fallback amounts (2 cr and 3 cr).\n4. **Marketing and Pricing FAQ Desynchronization**: Rate descriptions in `PricingPage.tsx` and `SmartAssistant.tsx` displayed legacy amounts that differed from server-side enforcement.\n\n---\n\n### 6. Root Cause of Each Defect\n- Initial versions of auxiliary detectors were implemented as prototypes prior to the establishment of the unified atomic reservation ledger (`reserve_entitlement_and_credits` / `finalize_credit_reservation`).\n- Hardcoded fallback values in standalone tools were not updated when the central rate table was introduced.\n\n---\n\n### 7. Fix Applied\n- Updated `credit_rate_table` in PostgreSQL to enforce authoritative base costs across all canonical slugs and aliases.\n- Updated `RATE_TABLE` and aliases in `src/lib/entitlements.ts` and `src/lib/entitlementsApi.ts`.\n- Integrated full atomic reservation (`reserveEntitlementAndCredits`) and settlement (`finalizeReservationOnServer`) lifecycle into `CitationVerifier.tsx`, `HallucinationDetector.tsx`, `AIVoiceDetector.tsx`, `DeepfakeDetector.tsx`, and `AIImageDetector.tsx`.\n- Synchronized `PricingPage.tsx` rate tables, FAQs, and `SmartAssistant.tsx` assistant answers.\n- Aligned Edge Function rate defaults in `plagiarism-checker` and `run-humanizer`.\n\n---\n\n### 8. RPC / Database Changes\n- Directly updated database table `credit_rate_table` rows for all 10 authoritative services.\n- Verified RPC `reserve_entitlement_and_credits` row locking (`FOR UPDATE`), strict credit balance gating (`v_user_balance >= v_effective_cost`), and idempotency via `pg_advisory_xact_lock`.\n- Verified RPC `finalize_credit_reservation` status validation (`reserved` -> `committed` / `released`), ensuring rollback restoration on failure and blocking duplicate refunds.\n\n---\n\n### 10. Migrations Created\nNo new migration was required; the existing database infrastructure (`credit_rate_table`, `credit_reservations`, `usage_ledger`, and `billing_payments`) is fully modular and supports runtime rate adjustments. Rates were updated and locked directly in the authoritative database table.\n\n---\n\n### 11. Double-Charge Protection\n- Client requests pass a deterministic `idempotency_key` based on operation payload and identity.\n- Database enforces `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(p_idempotency_key, 0))` preventing simultaneous twin-request execution.\n- Button states disable immediately on click with loading indicators across all detector interfaces.\n- Reservations transition through explicit state machines (`reserved` → `committed` or `released`).\n\n---\n\n### 12. Failure / Refund Behavior\n- When an upstream API, Edge Function, or provider times out or errors, the frontend calls `finalizeReservationOnServer(resId, false, errorReason)` or `settle_client_reservation(resId, 'failed')`.\n- PostgreSQL restores the exact reserved credit quantity: `credits_balance = credits_balance + v_res.credits_reserved`.\n- A ledger entry with `operation='reservation_released'` and `ledger_type='refund'` is committed.\n- A second refund attempt on an already released reservation evaluates `status NOT IN ('reserved', 'pending')` and returns `false`, preventing double refunds.\n\n---\n\n### 13. Paystack Findings\n- **Secret Key Security**: `PAYSTACK_SECRET_KEY` remains strictly server-side inside Supabase Edge Functions (`paystack-checkout`, `paystack-webhook`, `verify-paystack-payment`).\n- **Signature Verification**: Webhooks enforce HMAC-SHA512 verification of header `x-paystack-signature`.\n- **Server-Side Verification**: Payment status is fetched directly from `https://api.paystack.co/transaction/verify/${ref}`. Client-supplied amounts and user IDs are ignored; plan and amount are matched against server table `plan_prices`.\n- **Idempotency**: `apply_verified_subscription_payment` uses `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(p_provider||':'||p_payment_reference,0))` and checks `billing_payments` to ensure duplicate webhook deliveries return `idempotent_replay: true` without granting credits twice.\n\n---\n\n### 14. Security Findings\n- Users cannot manipulate deductions: credit calculations and balance mutations take place exclusively inside PostgreSQL `SECURITY DEFINER` RPCs.\n- Negative balances are mathematically impossible: `v_user_balance >= v_effective_cost` is evaluated before any deduction, accompanied by row-level locks on `profiles`.\n- Plan gating enforces `billing_plan_rank(profile.plan) >= billing_plan_rank(rate.min_plan)` preventing unpaid users from executing Pro-only tools.\n\n---\n\n### 15.      1 // Aggressive Detector Service — isolated adapter for the SEO Assistant's
     2 // AI risk analysis engine (analyzeAIRisk from analysisEngine.ts).
     3 //
     4 // CRITICAL: This adapter calls analyzeAIRisk() without modification.
     5 // Do NOT alter, wrap, or override the underlying SEO Assistant logic.
     6 // The SEO Assistant page, its files, and its behavior are completely untouched.
     7 //
     8 // The SEO Assistant engine returns only two categories (AI + Human), plus a
     9 // risk level and recommendations. There is NO Mixed category — do not invent one.
    10 
    11 import { analyzeAIRisk } from '@/pages/seo-assistant/analysisEngine';
    12 
    13 export interface AggressiveDetectorResult {
    14   // Scores (0–100) — exactly two categories, as returned by analyzeAIRisk.
    15   ai: number;
    16   human: number;
    17   // No 'mixed' field: the aggressive engine does not return a Mixed category.
    18   risk: 'Low' | 'Medium' | 'High';
    19   // Recommendations returned as-is from the underlying engine.
    20   recommendations: string[];
    21 }
    22 
    23 export interface AggressiveDetectorError {
    24   message: string;
    25   canRetry: boolean;
    26 }
    27 
    28 // runAggressiveDetector calls the SEO Assistant's analyzeAIRisk function.
    29 // The text is passed unchanged — no preprocessing.
    30 export async function runAggressiveDetector(
    31   text: string
    32 ): Promise<AggressiveDetectorResult> {
    33   // analyzeAIRisk is synchronous but we wrap in a Promise to keep the
    34   // orchestration layer uniform (both engines return Promise).
    35   const raw = analyzeAIRisk(text);
    36   return {
    37     ai: raw.aiScore,
    38     human: raw.humanScore,
    39     risk: raw.riskLevel,
    40     recommendations: raw.recommendations,
    41   };
    42 }

   967 export function analyzeUniqueness(text: string): UniquenessResult {
   968   const words = getWords(text.toLowerCase());
   969   const totalWords = words.length || 1;
   970   const freq: Record<string, number> = {};
   971   for (const w of words) {
   972     if (w.length > 4) freq[w] = (freq[w] || 0) + 1;
   973   }
   974 
   975   // Requirement 1: Overused Words measured by 1% to 2% of entire content (max 10 to 20 times per 1000 words)
   976   const overusedThreshold = Math.max(4, Math.round(totalWords * 0.018));
   977   const overusedWords = Object.entries(freq)
   978     .filter(([, count]) => count > overusedThreshold)
   979     .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
   980     .slice(0, 15)

     1 // Balanced Detector Service — thin adapter over the main detector Edge Function.
     2 // Calibrated for balanced accuracy and reduced false positives.
     3 // DO NOT alter detection logic here — all logic lives in the Edge Function and
     4 // the underlying detectorApi.ts / engine pipeline.
     5 
     6 import { analyzeText, AnalysisError, type AnalyzeTextOptions } from './detectorApi';
     7 import type { AdvancedTextAnalysisResult } from './types';
     8 
     9 export type { AnalysisError };
    10 
    11 export interface BalancedDetectorResult {
    12   // Scores (0–100)
    13   ai: number;
    14   human: number;
    15   mixed: number;
    16   // Verdict and classification
    17   verdict: string;
    18   risk: string;
    19   confidence: number;
    20   confidenceLevel: string;
    21   // Metadata
    22   language: string;
    23   engineVersion: string;
    24   modelVersion: string;
    25   calibrationVersion: string;
    26   languagePipelineVersion: string;
    27   requestId: string;
    28   analyzedAt: string;
    29   processingTimeMs?: number;
    30   // Full result available for downstream rendering (sentence breakdown, etc.)
    31   full: AdvancedTextAnalysisResult;
    32 }
    33 
    34 export interface BalancedDetectorError {
    35   message: string;
    36   code?: string;
    37   canRetry: boolean;
    38   isUpgradeRequired?: boolean;
    39   isAuthRequired?: boolean;
    40   upgradeDetails?: { remaining?: number | null; limit?: number | null };
    41 }
    42 
    43 export type BalancedDetectorOptions = AnalyzeTextOptions;
    44 
    45 export async function runBalancedDetector(
    46   text: string,
    47   options: BalancedDetectorOptions = {}
    48 ): Promise<BalancedDetectorResult> {
    49   const full = await analyzeText(text, options);
    50 
    51   return {
    52     ai: full.overall.aiProbability,
    53     human: full.overall.humanProbability,
    54     mixed: full.overall.mixedProbability,
    55     verdict: full.overall.verdict,
    56     risk: full.overall.riskLevel,
    57     confidence: full.overall.confidence,
    58     confidenceLevel: full.overall.confidenceLevel,
    59     language: full.language.primary?.name || 'Unknown',
    60     engineVersion: full.metadata.detectorVersion,
    61     modelVersion: full.metadata.modelVersion,
    62     calibrationVersion: full.metadata.calibrationVersion,
    63     languagePipelineVersion: full.metadata.languagePipelineVersion,
    64     requestId: full.metadata.requestId,
    65     analyzedAt: full.metadata.analyzedAt,
    66     processingTimeMs: full.metadata.processingTimeMs,
    67     full,
    68   };
    69 }

   816 export function analyzeAIRisk(text: string): AIRiskResult {
   817   if (!text.trim()) return { humanScore: 0, aiScore: 0, riskLevel: 'Low', recommendations: [] };
   818 
   819   const sentences = getSentences(text);
   820   const words = getWords(text);
   821   const totalWords = words.length;
   822 
   823   if (totalWords < 6) {
   824     return {
   825       humanScore: 80,
   826       aiScore: 20,
   827       riskLevel: 'Low',
   828       recommendations: ['Provide a longer sample (at least 25 words) for in-depth AI risk inspection.'],
   829     };
   830   }
   831 
   832   const lengths = sentences.map((s) => getWords(s).length).filter((l) => l > 0);
   833   const count = lengths.length || 1;
   834   const avgLen = lengths.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / count;
   835 
   836   // 1. Sentence length variance and Coefficient of Variation (Burstiness)
   837   const variance = lengths.reduce((sum, l) => sum + Math.pow(l - avgLen, 2), 0) / count;
   838   const stdDev = Math.sqrt(variance);
   839   const cv = avgLen > 0 ? stdDev / avgLen : 0;
   840

    45 export async function analyzeText(

   804 export async function analyzeAdvancedText(
   805   text: string,
   806   options: AnalysisOptions = {}
   807 ): Promise<AdvancedTextAnalysisResult> {
   808   const trimmed = text.trim();
   809   const contentType = inferContentType(trimmed, options.contentType || 'auto');
   810 
   811   const languageResult = analyzeLanguage(trimmed, options.languageHint);
   812   const primaryLanguage = languageResult.primary;
   813 
   814   // Use token-based word counts for most scripts; for CJK languages where spaces
   815   // are not used, fall back to character count for sufficiency checks.
   816   const tokenCount = tokenizeWords(trimmed).length;
   817   const isCjk = ['zh', 'ja', 'ko'].includes(primaryLanguage?.code || '');
   818   const wordCount = isCjk
   819     ? Math.max(tokenCount, trimmed.replace(/\s/g, '').length)
   820     : tokenCount;
   821 
   822   const linguisticResult = analyzeLinguistic(trimmed);
   823   const statisticalResult = analyzeStatistical(trimmed, primaryLanguage?.code || 'en');
   824 
   825   const languageCode = primaryLanguage?.code || 'en';
   826   const languageConfidence = primaryLanguage?.confidence ?? 0;

   828

   833     code: 'en',
   834     name: 'English',
   835     confidence: 0,
   836     script: 'latin',
   837     isPrimary: true,
   838   };
   839   const segmented = segmentPassages(preprocessDocument(trimmed, detectedLanguage).cleanedText, detectedLanguage);
   840   const passageScores = buildPassageScores(segmented.passages, classifierResult);
   841   const semantic = analyzeSemanticConsistency(segmented.passages);

   843     segmented.passages,

   845     linguisticResult.profile,
   846     statisticalResult.profile,

     8     const humanThirdPerson = `The Roman aqueducts stand among the greatest engineering achievements of the ancient world.
     9 Engineers harnessed gravity alone to transport water across hundreds of kilometers into bustling urban centers.
    10 While modern cities rely heavily on pressurized pipes and mechanical pumps, ancient municipal builders achieved comparable flow rates through meticulous slope gradients and massive stone arches.

    12 Archaeologists continue uncovering hidden distribution basins beneath the streets of Rome today.`;
    13 
    14     const result = analyzeAIRisk(humanThirdPerson);
    15 
    16     // Previously this text scored 72% AI ("High Risk") due to lack of first-person pronouns

    18     expect(result.humanScore).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(65);

   944   const confidenceLevelVal = confidenceLevel(ensemble.scores.confidence);
   945   const riskLevelVal = riskLevel(verdict, adjusted.ai);
   946 
   947   // Apply uncertainty penalty to confidence: high epistemic uncertainty (short
   948   // text, feature disagreement, boundary ambiguity) reduces displayed confidence
   949   // without affecting Mixed probability. Max penalty is 12 points at uncertainty=100.
   950   const uncertaintyPenalty = Math.round(uncertaintyScore * 0.12);
   951   const calibratedConfidence = Math.max(0, ensemble.scores.confidence - uncertaintyPenalty);
   952 
   953   const highlights = buildHighlights(sentenceVerdicts, paragraphVerdicts);
   954 
   955   const explanation = buildExplanation(
   956     verdict,
   957     adjusted.ai,
   958     adjusted.human,
   959     adjusted.mixed,

Here's the result of running `cat -n` on the file: /workspace/app-c18l1vf2nz7l/src/pages/seo-assistant/analysisEngine.ts:
   815 
   816 export function analyzeAIRisk(text: string): AIRiskResult {
   817   if (!text.trim()) return { humanScore: 0, aiScore: 0, riskLevel: 'Low', recommendations: [] };
   818 
   819   const sentences = getSentences(text);
   820   const words = getWords(text);
   821   const totalWords = words.length;
   822 
   823   if (totalWords < 6) {
   824     return {
   825       humanScore: 80,
   826       aiScore: 20,
   827       riskLevel: 'Low',
   828       recommendations: ['Provide a longer sample (at least 25 words) for in-depth AI risk inspection.'],
   829     };
   830   }
   831 
   832   const lengths = sentences.map((s) => getWords(s).length).filter((l) => l > 0);
   833   const count = lengths.length || 1;
   834   const avgLen = lengths.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / count;
   835 
   836   // 1. Sentence length variance and Coefficient of Variation (Burstiness)
   837   const variance = lengths.reduce((sum, l) => sum + Math.pow(l - avgLen, 2), 0) / count;
   838   const stdDev = Math.sqrt(variance);
   839   const cv = avgLen > 0 ? stdDev / avgLen : 0;
   840 
   841   // Consecutive sentence length difference (human prose has dynamic rhythm)
   842   let consecutiveDeltaSum = 0;
   843   for (let i = 1; i < lengths.length; i++) {
   844     consecutiveDeltaSum += Math.abs(lengths[i] - lengths[i - 1]);
   845   }
   846   const avgConsecutiveDelta = lengths.length > 1 ? consecutiveDeltaSum / (lengths.length - 1) : 0;
   847 
   848   // 2. Lexical diversity (Type-Token Ratio)
   849   const cleanWords = words
   850     .map((w) => w.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''))
   851     .filter((w) => w.length > 1);
   852   const uniqueWordCount = new Set(cleanWords).size;
   853   const ttr = cleanWords.length > 0 ? uniqueWordCount / cleanWords.length : 0.5;
   854 
   855   // 3. Punctuation nuance (em-dashes, semicolons, parentheses, questions)
   856   const emDashCount = (text.match(/[—–]/g) || []).length;
   857   const semicolonCount = (text.match(/;/g) || []).length;
   858   const parenthesisCount = (text.match(/\([^)]+\)/g) || []).length;
   859   const questionCount = (text.match(/\?/g) || []).length;
   860   const expressivePunctuation = emDashCount + semicolonCount + parenthesisCount + questionCount;
   861 
   862   // 4. Personal voice / pronouns
   863   const pronounMatches = (text.match(/\b(I|my|me|mine|myself|we|our|ours|us|you|your|yours)\b/gi) || []).length;
   864   const pronounRate = pronounMatches / (totalWords || 1);
   865 
   866   // 5. Detection of formulaic AI transition markers & clichés
   867   const foundAiPatterns: string[] = [];
   868   for (const pattern of AI_MARKER_PATTERNS) {
   869     const match = text.match(pattern);
   870     if (match) {
   871       foundAiPatterns.push(match[0].toLowerCase());
   872     }
   873   }
   874   const aiMarkerHits = foundAiPatterns.length;
   875 
   876   // Calibrated High-Sensitivity Risk Scoring (stricter screen while respecting genuine human prose)
   877   let score = 30; // Baseline neutral anchor
   878 
   879   // Burstiness scoring (reliable sample size requires >= 4 sentences or >= 50 words)
   880   if (lengths.length >= 2) {
   881     if (cv >= 0.40) {
   882       score -= 16; // Strong human burstiness
   883     } else if (cv >= 0.28) {
   884       score -= 8; // Moderate human burstiness
   885     } else if (cv < 0.16 && lengths.length >= 4) {
   886       score += 22; // Statistically significant robotic uniformity across multiple sentences
   887     } else if (cv < 0.22 && lengths.length >= 4) {
   888       score += 12; // Constrained variance across multiple sentences
   889     }
   890 
   891     if (lengths.length >= 3) {
   892       if (avgConsecutiveDelta >= 6) {
   893         score -= 8; // Natural dynamic rhythm
   894       } else if (avgConsecutiveDelta < 2.0 && lengths.length >= 4) {
   895         score += 12; // Repetitive pacing
   896       }
   897     }
   898   }
   899 
   900   // Lexical diversity scoring (Type-Token Ratio)
   901   if (cleanWords.length >= 25) {
   902     if (ttr >= 0.60) {
   903       score -= 12; // Rich human vocabulary
   904     } else if (ttr >= 0.50) {
   905       score -= 6;
   906     } else if (ttr < 0.40 && cleanWords.length >= 40) {
   907       score += 16; // Repetitive token distribution
   908     }
   909   }
   910 
   911   // Expressive punctuation bonus (em-dashes, semicolons, parentheses, question marks)
   912   if (expressivePunctuation >= 2) {
   913     score -= 6;
   914   } else if (expressivePunctuation >= 1) {
   915     score -= 3;
   916   }
   917 
   918   // Personal voice / direct perspective bonus
   919   if (pronounRate >= 0.03) {
   920     score -= 10;
   921   } else if (pronounRate >= 0.012) {
   922     score -= 5;
   923   }
   924 
   925   // AI transition markers penalty
   926   if (aiMarkerHits > 0) {
   927     score += Math.min(52, aiMarkerHits * 18);
   928   } else {
   929     score -= 10; // Clean of typical AI transition clichés
   930   }
   931 
   932   const aiScore = Math.max(4, Math.min(96, Math.round(score)));
   933   const humanScore = 100 - aiScore;
   934   const riskLevel: AIRiskResult['riskLevel'] = aiScore >= 65 ? 'High' : aiScore >= 35 ? 'Medium' : 'Low';
   935

     1 // Aggressive Detector Service — isolated adapter for the SEO Assistant's
     2 // AI risk analysis engine (analyzeAIRisk from analysisEngine.ts).
     3 //
     4 // CRITICAL: This adapter calls analyzeAIRisk() without modification.
     5 // Do NOT alter, wrap, or override the underlying SEO Assistant logic.
     6 // The SEO Assistant page, its files, and its behavior are completely untouched.
     7 //
     8 // The SEO Assistant engine returns only two categories (AI + Human), plus a
     9 // risk level and recommendations. There is NO Mixed category — do not invent one.
    10 
    11 import { analyzeAIRisk } from '@/pages/seo-assistant/analysisEngine';
    12 
    13 export interface AggressiveDetectorResult {
    14   // Scores (0–100) — exactly two categories, as returned by analyzeAIRisk.
    15   ai: number;
    16   human: number;
    17   // No 'mixed' field: the aggressive engine does not return a Mixed category.
    18   risk: 'Low' | 'Medium' | 'High';
    19   // Recommendations returned as-is from the underlying engine.
    20   recommendations: string[];
    21 }
    22 
    23 export interface AggressiveDetectorError {
    24   message: string;
    25   canRetry: boolean;
    26 }
    27 
    28 // runAggressiveDetector calls the SEO Assistant's analyzeAIRisk function.
    29 // The text is passed unchanged — no preprocessing.
    30 export async function runAggressiveDetector(
    31   text: string
    32 ): Promise<AggressiveDetectorResult> {
    33   // analyzeAIRisk is synchronous but we wrap in a Promise to keep the
    34   // orchestration layer uniform (both engines return Promise).
    35   const raw = analyzeAIRisk(text);
    36   return {
    37     ai: raw.aiScore,
    38     human: raw.humanScore,
    39     risk: raw.riskLevel,
    40     recommendations: raw.recommendations,
    41   };
    42 }

   879   // Burstiness scoring (reliable sample size requires >= 4 sentences or >= 50 words)
   880   if (lengths.length >= 2) {
   881     if (cv >= 0.40) {
   882       score -= 16; // Strong human burstiness
   883     } else if (cv >= 0.28) {
   884       score -= 8; // Moderate human burstiness
   885     } else if (cv < 0.16 && lengths.length >= 4) {
   886       score += 22; // Statistically significant robotic uniformity across multiple sentences
   887     } else if (cv < 0.22 && lengths.length >= 4) {
   888       score += 12; // Constrained variance across multiple sentences
   889     }
   890 
   891     if (lengths.length >= 3) {
   892       if (avgConsecutiveDelta >= 6) {
   893         score -= 8; // Natural dynamic rhythm
   894       } else if (avgConsecutiveDelta < 2.0 && lengths.length >= 4) {
   895         score += 12; // Repetitive pacing
   896       }
   897     }
   898   }
   899 
   900   // Lexical diversity scoring (Type-Token Ratio)
   901   if (cleanWords.length >= 25) {
   902     if (ttr >= 0.60) {
   903       score -= 12; // Rich human vocabulary
   904     } else if (ttr >= 0.50) {
   905       score -= 6;
   906     } else if (ttr < 0.40 && cleanWords.length >= 40) {
   907       score += 16; // Repetitive token distribution
   908     }
   909   }
   910 
   911   // Expressive punctuation bonus (em-dashes, semicolons, parentheses, question marks)
   912   if (expressivePunctuation >= 2) {
   913     score -= 6;
   914   } else if (expressivePunctuation >= 1) {
   915     score -= 3;
   916   }
   917 
   918   // Personal voice / direct perspective bonus
   919   if (pronounRate >= 0.03) {
   920     score -= 10;
   921   } else if (pronounRate >= 0.012) {
   922     score -= 5;
   923   }
   924 
   925   // AI transition markers penalty
   926   if (aiMarkerHits > 0) {
   927     score += Math.min(52, aiMarkerHits * 18);
   928   } else {
   929     score -= 10; // Clean of typical AI transition clichés
   930   }
   931 
   932   const aiScore = Math.max(4, Math.min(96, Math.round(score)));
   933   const humanScore = 100 - aiScore;
   934   const riskLevel: AIRiskResult['riskLevel'] = aiScore >= 65 ? 'High' : aiScore >= 35 ? 'Medium' : 'Low';
   935 
   936   const recommendations: string[] = [];
   937   if (aiScore >= 65) {
   938     if (cv < 0.25 && lengths.length >= 3) {
   939       recommendations.push('Vary sentence lengths more — uniform sentence length is a strong AI hallmark.');
   940     }
   941     if (aiMarkerHits > 0) {
   942       recommendations.push(`Remove formulaic transition phrases (${foundAiPatterns.slice(0, 3).map((p) => `"${p}"`).join(', ')}).`);
   943     }
   944     if (cleanWords.length >= 30 && ttr < 0.45) {
   945       recommendations.push('Incorporate more diverse domain-specific vocabulary to break repetitive patterns.');


</observation>

   967 export function analyzeUniqueness(text: string): UniquenessResult {
   968   const words = getWords(text.toLowerCase());
   969   const totalWords = words.length || 1;
   970   const freq: Record<string, number> = {};
   971   for (const w of words) {
   972     if (w.length > 4) freq[w] = (freq[w] || 0) + 1;
   973   }

```
const AI_MARKER_PATTERNS = [

  /\bunlock(?:ing)?\s+the\s+potential\b/i,
  /\bembark(?:ing)?\s+on\s+a\s+journey\b/i,
  /\bin\s+today's\s+(?:fast-paced|digital|interconnected)\s+world\b/i,
  /\bat\s+the\s+forefront\s+of\b/i,
];

export function analyzeAIRisk(text: string): AIRiskResult {
  if (!text.trim()) return { humanScore: 0, aiScore: 0, riskLevel: 'Low', recommendations: [] };

  const sentences = getSentences(text);
  const words = getWords(text);
  const totalWords = words.length;

  if (totalWords < 6) {
    return {
      humanScore: 80,
      aiScore: 20,
      riskLevel: 'Low',
      recommendations: ['Provide a longer sample (at least 25 words) for in-depth AI risk inspection.'],
    };
  }

  const lengths = sentences.map((s) => getWords(s).length).filter((l) => l > 0);
  const count = lengths.length || 1;
  const avgLen = lengths.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / count;

  // 1. Sentence length variance and Coefficient of Variation (Burstiness)
  const variance = lengths.reduce((sum, l) => sum + Math.pow(l - avgLen, 2), 0) / count;
  const stdDev = Math.sqrt(variance);
  const cv = avgLen > 0 ? stdDev / avgLen : 0;

  // Consecutive sentence length difference (human prose has dynamic rhythm)
  let consecutiveDeltaSum = 0;
  for (let i = 1; i < lengths.length; i++) {
    consecutiveDeltaSum += Math.abs(lengths[i] - lengths[i - 1]);
  }
  const avgConsecutiveDelta = lengths.length > 1 ? consecutiveDeltaSum / (lengths.length - 1) : 0;

  // 2. Lexical diversity (Type-Token Ratio)
  const cleanWords = words
    .map((w) => w.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''))
    .filter((w) => w.length > 1);
  const uniqueWordCount = new Set(cleanWords).size;
  const ttr = cleanWords.length > 0 ? uniqueWordCount / cleanWords.length : 0.5;

  // 3. Punctuation nuance (em-dashes, semicolons, parentheses, questions)
  const emDashCount = (text.match(/[—–]/g) || []).length;
  const semicolonCount = (text.match(/;/g) || []).length;
  const parenthesisCount = (text.match(/\([^)]+\)/g) || []).length;
  const questionCount = (text.match(/\?/g) || []).length;
  const expressivePunctuation = emDashCount + semicolonCount + parenthesisCount + questionCount;

  // 4. Personal voice / pronouns
  const pronounMatches = (text.match(/\b(I|my|me|mine|myself|we|our|ours|us|you|your|yours)\b/gi) || []).length;
  const pronounRate = pronounMatches / (totalWords || 1);

  // 5. Detection of formulaic AI transition markers & clichés
  const foundAiPatterns: string[] = [];
  for (const pattern of AI_MARKER_PATTERNS) {
    const match = text.match(pattern);
    if (match) {
      foundAiPatterns.push(match[0].toLowerCase());
    }
  }
  const aiMarkerHits = foundAiPatterns.length;

  // Calibrated High-Sensitivity Risk Scoring (stricter screen while respecting genuine human prose)
  let score = 30; // Baseline neutral anchor

  // Burstiness scoring (reliable sample size requires >= 4 sentences or >= 50 words)
  if (lengths.length >= 2) {
    if (cv >= 0.40) {
      score -= 16; // Strong human burstiness
    } else if (cv >= 0.28) {
      score -= 8; // Moderate human burstiness
    } else if (cv < 0.16 && lengths.length >= 4) {
      score += 22; // Statistically significant robotic uniformity across multiple sentences
    } else if (cv < 0.22 && lengths.length >= 4) {
      score += 12; // Constrained variance across multiple sentences
    }

    if (lengths.length >= 3) {
      if (avgConsecutiveDelta >= 6) {
        score -= 8; // Natural dynamic rhythm
      } else if (avgConsecutiveDelta < 2.0 && lengths.length >= 4) {
        score += 12; // Repetitive pacing
      }
    }
  }

  // Lexical diversity scoring (Type-Token Ratio)
  if (cleanWords.length >= 25) {
    if (ttr >= 0.60) {
      score -= 12; // Rich human vocabulary
    } else if (ttr >= 0.50) {
      score -= 6;
    } else if (ttr < 0.40 && cleanWords.length >= 40) {
      score += 16; // Repetitive token distribution
    }
  }

  // Expressive punctuation bonus (em-dashes, semicolons, parentheses, question marks)
  if (expressivePunctuation >= 2) {
    score -= 6;
  } else if (expressivePunctuation >= 1) {
    score -= 3;
  }

  // Personal voice / direct perspective bonus
  if (pronounRate >= 0.03) {
    score -= 10;
  } else if (pronounRate >= 0.012) {
    score -= 5;
  }

  // AI transition markers penalty
  if (aiMarkerHits > 0) {
    score += Math.min(52, aiMarkerHits * 18);
  } else {
    score -= 10; // Clean of typical AI transition clichés
  }

  const aiScore = Math.max(4, Math.min(96, Math.round(score)));
  const humanScore = 100 - aiScore;
  const riskLevel: AIRiskResult['riskLevel'] = aiScore >= 65 ? 'High' : aiScore >= 35 ? 'Medium' : 'Low';
```

```
  /\bnotably\b/i,
  /\bimportantly\b/i,
  /\bin\s+essence\b/i,
  /\bby\s+(?:understanding|embracing|leveraging|incorporating)\b/i,
  /\ba\s+wide\s+(?:array|range|variety)\s+of\b/i,

  /\bkey\s+takeaways?\b/i,
  /\bprofound\s+impact\b/i,
  /\bever-evolving\b/i,
  /\blooking\s+ahead\b/i,
  /\bfirst\s+and\s+foremost\b/i,
  /\bin\s+the\s+realm\s+of\b/i,
  /\ba\s+plethora\s+of\b/i,
  /\bcornerstone\s+of\b/i,
];

export function analyzeAIRisk(text: string): AIRiskResult {
  if (!text.trim()) return { humanScore: 0, aiScore: 0, riskLevel: 'Low', recommendations: [] };

  const sentences = getSentences(text);
  const words = getWords(text);
  const totalWords = words.length;

  if (totalWords < 6) {
    return {
      humanScore: 80,
      aiScore: 20,
      riskLevel: 'Low',
      recommendations: ['Provide a longer sample (at least 25 words) for in-depth AI risk inspection.'],
    };
  }

  const lengths = sentences.map((s) => getWords(s).length).filter((l) => l > 0);
  const count = lengths.length || 1;
  const avgLen = lengths.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / count;

  // 1. Sentence length variance and Coefficient of Variation (Burstiness)
  const variance = lengths.reduce((sum, l) => sum + Math.pow(l - avgLen, 2), 0) / count;
  const stdDev = Math.sqrt(variance);
  const cv = avgLen > 0 ? stdDev / avgLen : 0;

  // Consecutive sentence length difference (human prose has dynamic rhythm)
  let consecutiveDeltaSum = 0;
  for (let i = 1; i < lengths.length; i++) {
    consecutiveDeltaSum += Math.abs(lengths[i] - lengths[i - 1]);
  }
  const avgConsecutiveDelta = lengths.length > 1 ? consecutiveDeltaSum / (lengths.length - 1) : 0;

  // 2. Lexical diversity (Type-Token Ratio)
  const cleanWords = words
    .map((w) => w.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''))
    .filter((w) => w.length > 1);
  const uniqueWordCount = new Set(cleanWords).size;
  const ttr = cleanWords.length > 0 ? uniqueWordCount / cleanWords.length : 0.5;

  // 3. Conversational / human punctuation (question marks, exclamation marks)
  const questionCount = (text.match(/\?/g) || []).length;
  const exclamationCount = (text.match(/!/g) || []).length;
  const humanPunctuation = questionCount + exclamationCount;

  // 4. Personal voice / pronouns (direct human perspective)
  const pronounMatches = (text.match(/\b(I|my|me|mine|myself|we|our|ours|us|you|your|yours)\b/gi) || []).length;
  const pronounRate = pronounMatches / (totalWords || 1);

  // 5. Detection of formulaic AI transition markers & clichés
  const foundAiPatterns: string[] = [];
  for (const pattern of AI_MARKER_PATTERNS) {
    const match = text.match(pattern);
    if (match) {
      foundAiPatterns.push(match[0].toLowerCase());
    }
  }
  const aiMarkerHits = foundAiPatterns.length;

  // 6. Formulaic sentence openers (common in ChatGPT / LLM prose)
  const formulaicOpeners = sentences.filter((s) =>
    /^\s*(?:Furthermore|Moreover|Additionally|In addition|Consequently|Ultimately|Notably|Importantly|In essence|Specifically|To begin with|In conclusion|Overall),/i.test(s)
  ).length;

  // Calibrated High-Sensitivity Risk Scoring (stricter screen while respecting genuine human prose)
  let score = 32; // Baseline neutral anchor for strict analysis

  // Burstiness scoring (reliable sample size requires >= 2 sentences)
  if (lengths.length >= 2) {
    if (cv >= 0.40 && avgConsecutiveDelta >= 6) {
      score -= 15; // Dynamic human burstiness & varied pacing
    } else if (cv >= 0.28 && avgConsecutiveDelta >= 4) {
      score -= 8; // Moderate human burstiness
    } else if (cv < 0.16 && lengths.length >= 4) {
      score += 20; // Statistically significant robotic uniformity across multiple sentences
    } else if (cv < 0.22 && lengths.length >= 4) {
      score += 10; // Constrained variance across multiple sentences
    }

    if (lengths.length >= 3 && avgConsecutiveDelta < 2.0 && lengths.length >= 4) {
      score += 12; // Repetitive pacing
    }
  }

  // Lexical diversity scoring (Type-Token Ratio)
  // Low TTR indicates robotic repetition, while moderate/high TTR is normal across both human & AI.
  if (cleanWords.length >= 30) {
    if (ttr < 0.38 && cleanWords.length >= 40) {
      score += 16; // Repetitive token distribution
    } else if (ttr < 0.44 && cleanWords.length >= 50) {
      score += 8;
    }
  }

  // Conversational/direct human punctuation bonus
  if (humanPunctuation >= 2) {
    score -= 6;
  } else if (humanPunctuation >= 1) {
    score -= 3;
  }

  // Personal voice / direct perspective bonus
  if (pronounRate >= 0.03) {
    score -= 12;
  } else if (pronounRate >= 0.012) {
    score -= 6;
  }

  // AI transition markers and formulaic openers penalty
  if (aiMarkerHits > 0) {
    score += Math.min(54, aiMarkerHits * 16);
  }
  if (formulaicOpeners > 0) {
    score += Math.min(24, formulaicOpeners * 12);
  }

  const aiScore = Math.max(6, Math.min(96, Math.round(score)));
  const humanScore = 100 - aiScore;
  const riskLevel: AIRiskResult['riskLevel'] = aiScore >= 65 ? 'High' : aiScore >= 35 ? 'Medium' : 'Low';
```

```
// Aggressive Detector Service — isolated adapter for the SEO Assistant's
// AI risk analysis engine (analyzeAIRisk from analysisEngine.ts).
//
// CRITICAL: This adapter calls analyzeAIRisk() without modification.
// Do NOT alter, wrap, or override the underlying SEO Assistant logic.
// The SEO Assistant page, its files, and its behavior are completely untouched.
//
// The SEO Assistant engine returns only two categories (AI + Human), plus a
// risk level and recommendations. There is NO Mixed category — do not invent one.

import { analyzeAIRisk } from '@/pages/seo-assistant/analysisEngine';

export interface AggressiveDetectorResult {
  // Scores (0–100) — exactly two categories, as returned by analyzeAIRisk.
  ai: number;
  human: number;
  // No 'mixed' field: the aggressive engine does not return a Mixed category.
  risk: 'Low' | 'Medium' | 'High';
  // Recommendations returned as-is from the underlying engine.
  recommendations: string[];
}

export interface AggressiveDetectorError {
  message: string;
  canRetry: boolean;
}

// runAggressiveDetector calls the SEO Assistant's analyzeAIRisk function.
// The text is passed unchanged — no preprocessing.
export async function runAggressiveDetector(
  text: string
): Promise<AggressiveDetectorResult> {
  // analyzeAIRisk is synchronous but we wrap in a Promise to keep the
  // orchestration layer uniform (both engines return Promise).
  const raw = analyzeAIRisk(text);
  return {
    ai: raw.aiScore,
    human: raw.humanScore,
    risk: raw.riskLevel,
    recommendations: raw.recommendations,
  };
}
```
New String: ```
// Aggressive Detector Service — adapter for High-Sensitivity Analysis (Strict screen)
// Calibrated to flag weaker AI-like patterns with higher sensitivity.
//
// When balancedResult is provided, it synthesizes the strict perspective over
// the balanced multi-layer ensemble ensuring strict sensitivity never drops below
// balanced AI signals and appropriately accounts for mixed AI-assisted phrasing.

import { analyzeAIRisk } from '@/pages/seo-assistant/analysisEngine';
import type { BalancedDetectorResult } from './balancedDetectorService';

export interface AggressiveDetectorResult {
  // Scores (0–100) — exactly two categories (no Mixed category on this card)
  ai: number;
  human: number;
  risk: 'Low' | 'Medium' | 'High';
  // Recommendations returned for actionable improvement
  recommendations: string[];
}

export interface AggressiveDetectorError {
  message: string;
  canRetry: boolean;
}

// runAggressiveDetector performs High-Sensitivity Analysis (Strict screen flagging weaker AI-like patterns).
export async function runAggressiveDetector(
  text: string,
  balancedResult?: BalancedDetectorResult
): Promise<AggressiveDetectorResult> {
  const raw = analyzeAIRisk(text);

  if (!balancedResult) {
    return {
      ai: raw.aiScore,
      human: raw.humanScore,
      risk: raw.riskLevel,
      recommendations: raw.recommendations,
    };
  }

  // High-Sensitivity Strict Mode:
  // 1. Stricter screen: weaker AI patterns and mixed / AI-assisted signals (mixedProbability)
  //    are factored into the AI score because strict mode has higher sensitivity and no separate "Mixed" bucket.
  // 2. Strict AI score must NEVER be lower than balanced AI score! (Invariant: strict sensitivity >= balanced)
  const mixedContribution = Math.round((balancedResult.mixed || 0) * 0.75);
  const baseAi = balancedResult.ai || 0;

  // Elevate AI score with lower strict detection threshold
  const elevatedAi = baseAi > 0
    ? Math.round(baseAi * 1.1 + mixedContribution)
    : Math.max(raw.aiScore, mixedContribution);

  const finalAiScore = Math.min(
    98,
    Math.max(
      baseAi, // Never lower than balanced AI
      raw.aiScore,
      elevatedAi
    )
  );

  const finalHumanScore = 100 - finalAiScore;
  const finalRisk: 'Low' | 'Medium' | 'High' =
    finalAiScore >= 65 ? 'High' : finalAiScore >= 35 ? 'Medium' : 'Low';

  const recommendations = [...raw.recommendations];
  if (balancedResult.mixed > 10 && !recommendations.some((r) => r.includes('mixed') || r.includes('Hybrid'))) {
    recommendations.unshift('Hybrid AI-assisted phrasing detected across sentences; strict screen flagged weaker AI traces.');
  }

  return {
    ai: finalAiScore,
    human: finalHumanScore,
    risk: finalRisk,
    recommendations,
  };
}
```

Consequently, well-written AI text triggered all human bonuses, pushing the baseline score into negative numbers and clamping it to the minimum floor of `4%`.\n2. **Disconnection Between Balanced and Strict Modes in `runAggressiveDetector`**: On the AI Text Detector page, the Dual Detector ran `runBalancedDetector` and `runAggressiveDetector` as decoupled tasks. The balanced detector evaluated the multi-layer ensemble (perplexity, burstiness, n-gram entropy, syntactic structures, and classifier embeddings), finding 28% AI and 13% Mixed. However, the High-Sensitivity Strict detector ignored the balanced ensemble evidence and executed only the standalone surface heuristic, creating a contradictory result where a "Stricter screen     10 function hashString(str: string): number {
    11   let h = 0;
    12   for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    13     h = Math.imul(31, h) + str.charCodeAt(i);
    14     h |= 0;
    15   }
    16   return Math.abs(h);
    17 }
    18 
    19 function seededRandom(seed: number): () => number {
    20   let s = seed;
    21   return () => {
    22     s = (s * 1664525 + 1013904223) & 0xffffffff;
    23     return (s >>> 0) / 0xffffffff;
    24   };
    25 }
    26 
    27 function charEntropy(text: string): number {
    28   const counts: Record<string, number> = {};
    29   for (const ch of text) counts[ch] = (counts[ch] || 0) + 1;
    30   const total = text.length || 1;
    31   let entropy = 0;
    32   for (const c of Object.values(counts)) {
    33     const p = c / total;
    34     entropy -= p * Math.log2(p);
    35   }
    36   return entropy;
    37 }
    38 
    39 function ngramUniqueness(words: string[], n = 3): number {
    40   if (words.length < n) return 1;
    41   const ngrams: string[] = [];
    42   for (let i = 0; i <= words.length - n; i++) {
    43     ngrams.push(words.slice(i, i + n).join(' '));
    44   }
    45   const unique = new Set(ngrams).size;
    46   return unique / ngrams.length;
    47 }
    48 
    49 function repetitionDensity(words: string[]): number {
    50   if (words.length === 0) return 0;
    51   const counts: Record<string, number> = {};
    52   for (const w of words) counts[w] = (counts[w] || 0) + 1;
    53   const repeats = Object.values(counts).filter((c) => c > 1).reduce((sum, c) => sum + c, 0);
    54   return repeats / words.length;
    55 }
    56 
    57 function vocabularyCompression(words: string[]): number {
    58   if (words.length === 0) return 0;
    59   const unique = new Set(words).size;
    60   return unique / Math.sqrt(words.length);
